任务代号 GGGG（用户实战报障）。终极目标：JS 网页版泰拉瑞亚世界生成与原版 1.4.5.6 逐位一致。

## 用户报障
"世界生成时仍然会生成没有树根的树——树木最底下不是泥土而是花草"（seed 12345 小世界）。

## 协调方已实测的数据（探针已删，可重建：generateWorld seed 12345 + parseWld /tmp/vserver86/s12345.wld 同口径逐列扫树干 sheet 5）
- **x86 原版树底地面格只有实心族**：2 草×99 / 60 丛草×66 / 199 猩红草×26 / 70 泥×23 / 147 雪×17——**从不站在植物(73)/花(3)上**。
- **JS 有 ~23 列树站在 73@fx（植物，帧 378-738 各种）/ 3@fx（花）上**——树浮在装饰植物上方一格 = 用户看到的"无根树"。
- 另有树列错位（JS 206 列 vs x86 231，仅 17 列同位同帧）——这是尾段上游债级联（树=槽82；槽 54-105 债由在途批 CCCC/DDDD/EEEE/FFFF 清偿中，树 pass 本身已被 WWW 反事实裁决零自因），**不归你修**；你只修"花草垫底"这一类。
- 底部干格帧分布两侧也不同——随列错位而来，不单独定性。

## 待裁决假说（按可能性排序）
1. **树干底部被抬高一格**：Trees pass（cs:20085）基座锚点差 1（干底本应在草地上一格，实际在两格）→ 草顶空格在 GrassPlants（cs:20232，门=!active 上方）合法填植物 → "花草垫树"。
2. **干底格事后被杀**：某 pass（ Flowers cs:20592？QuickCleanup？）KillTile 干底格使整干上浮一格。★注意 memory 里有一条旧裁决"树底格被草占=原版行为——Flowers pass(在 Trees 后) KillTile 树干底格+放短草"（tree-bottom-grass-overwrite.md）：但 x86 实测原版树底从无 73/3——**旧裁决须复核**（可能是对"底格"定义不同=地面格被短草 61 类覆盖，而非干下空格）。
3. GrassPlants/AlchemyHerbs/染料植物某支对"上方活性"判定漏了树干（如只查 solid 不查 active）。

## 步骤
1. 重建探针复现（_ 前缀，跑完删）；对 23 列花草垫底树取证：干底 y、地面 y、格史（instrument 写/杀探针过滤这些列）定源 pass。
2. 查 Terarria1456/（Trees cs:20085 / GrassPlants cs:20232 / AlchemyHerbs cs:20109 / DyePlants cs:20126 / Flowers cs:20592）对应谓词 1:1 对账。
3. 修复；复核旧裁决并更正 memory 文件（~/.claude/projects/-Users-user-Project-GLM-SandboxWorld/memory/tree-bottom-grass-overwrite.md）如结论有偏。
4. 验证：
   - 12345 重生成：树底地面格 hist 只剩实心族（对齐 x86 分类）；无 73/3 垫底；
   - `SW_M8=1 npx vitest run tools/_mile8.test.ts` 槽 0-53 全绿不回退；
   - `npx vitest run` 不新增红（陈旧金标红/在途批红不计）。
5. 报告追加 game/docs/worldgen/content-parity-vs-vanilla-2026-08-16.md 末章（GGGG 批），并把"树列错位清单"（x 坐标差集）留给终局复验。

## 纪律（硬约束）
- 金标零写入；/tmp/sw-slp/g/、/tmp/vserver86/ 只读；探针 _ 前缀跑完删（删前 pgrep）；单命令 <8min；禁 kill 5199；不动 vite.config.ts；vitest 无需 server；改前重 Read 现场。

- vitest 全量现存红=陈旧金标带+在途批+并发超时伪象，逐条归因即可。

## 交付

					}
					for (int j = num; j < Main.maxTilesY - 100; j++)

							int num4 = i + num2;

				}

	public static void CheckExploitDestroyQueue()
	{
		if (destroyObject)
		{
			return;
		}
		while (ExploitDestroyQueue.Count > 0)
		{
			Point point = ExploitDestroyQueue.Dequeue();
			if (Framing.GetTileSafely(point.X, point.Y).active())
			{
				SquareTileFrame(point.X, point.Y);
				if (Main.netMode != 0)
				{
					NetMessage.SendTileSquare(-1, point.X, point.Y);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private static Player GetPlayerForTile(int x, int y)
	{
		return Main.player[Player.FindClosest(new Vector2(x, y) * 16f, 16, 16)];
	}

	public static bool CheckTileBreakability2_ShouldTileSurvive(int x, int y)
	{
		if (Main.netMode == 1)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (Main.tile[x, y] == null)
		{
			Main.tile[x, y] = new Tile();
		}
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if (TileID.Sets.BasicChest[tile.type])
		{
			int num = tile.frameX / 18;
			int y2 = y - tile.frameY / 18;
			while (num > 1)
			{
				num -= 2;
			}
			num = x - num;
			if (!Chest.DestroyChest(num, y2))
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		if (tile.type == 88)
		{
			int num2 = tile.frameX / 18;
			int y3 = y - tile.frameY / 18;
			num2 %= 3;
			num2 = x - num2;
			if (!Chest.DestroyChest(num2, y3))
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		if (tile.type == 470)
		{
			return !TEDisplayDoll.IsBreakable(x, y);
		}
		if (tile.type == 475)
		{
			return !TEHatRack.IsBreakable(x, y);
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool ReplaceWall(int x, int y, ushort targetWall)
	{
		if (targetWall >= WallID.Count)
		{
			return false;
		}
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if (tile.wall == 0 || targetWall == 0 || tile.wall == 350)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (KillWall_CheckFailure(fail: false, tile))
		{
			return false;
		}
		int num = 10;
		for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
		{
			KillWall_MakeWallDust(x, y, tile);
		}
		KillWall_PlaySounds(x, y, tile);
		KillWall_DropItems(x, y, tile);
		tile.wall = targetWall;
		tile.ClearWallPaintAndCoating();
		SquareWallFrame(x, y);
		return true;
	}

	public static bool ReplaceTile(int x, int y, int targetType, int targetStyle)
	{
		Tile tileSafely = Framing.GetTileSafely(x, y);
		if (!WouldTileReplacementWork(targetType, x, y))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!IsTileReplaceable(x, y))
		{
			return false;
		}

	public static int CheckTileBreakability(int x, int y)
	{
		if (Main.tile[x, y] == null)
		{
			Main.tile[x, y] = new Tile();
		}
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if (y >= 1 && y <= Main.maxTilesY - 1)
		{
			if (Main.tile[x, y - 1] == null)
			{
				Main.tile[x, y - 1] = new Tile();
			}
			if (Main.tile[x, y + 1] == null)
			{
				Main.tile[x, y + 1] = new Tile();
			}
			Tile tile2 = Main.tile[x, y - 1];
			Tile tile3 = Main.tile[x, y + 1];
			if (tile3 != null && tile3.active() && IsLockedDoor(tile3))
			{
				return 2;
			}
			if (!Main.tileSolid[tile.type] && !Main.tileSolidTop[tile.type])
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (tile2.active())
			{
				bool flag = IsAContainer(tile);
				if (CheckTileBreakability_HasReasonToReturnEarly(tile.type, tile2, !flag))
				{
					if (TileID.Sets.IsATreeTrunk[tile2.type])
					{
						if ((tile2.frameX == 66 && tile2.frameY >= 0 && tile2.frameY <= 44) || (tile2.frameX == 88 && tile2.frameY >= 66 && tile2.frameY <= 110) || tile2.frameY >= 198)
						{
							return 0;
						}
						return 2;
					}
					if (tile2.type == 323)
					{
						if (tile2.frameX == 66 || tile2.frameX == 220)
						{
							return 2;
						}
						return 0;
					}
					return 2;
				}
				if (tile2.type == 80 && tile2.type != tile.type)
				{
					int num = tile2.frameX / 18;
					if ((uint)num <= 1u || (uint)(num - 4) <= 1u)
					{
						return 2;
					}
				}
				if (tile.type == 10 && IsLockedDoor(tile))
				{
					return 1;
				}
				if (TileID.Sets.Boulders[tile.type])
				{
					if (CheckBoulderChest(x, y))
					{
						return 1;
					}
					return 0;
				}
			}
			if (tile.type == 235)
			{
				int frameX = tile.frameX;
				int num2 = x - frameX % 54 / 18;
				for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
				{
					Tile tile4 = Main.tile[num2 + i, y - 1];
					if (tile4.active() && CheckTileBreakability_HasReasonToReturnEarly(tile.type, tile4, scanForContainer: true))
					{
						return 2;
					}
				}
			}
		}
		return 0;
	}

	private static bool CheckTileBreakability_HasReasonToReturnEarly(int ignoreType, Tile target, bool scanForContainer)
	{
		if (ignoreType != target.type)
		{
			if (target.type == 77 && !Main.hardMode)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (TileID.Sets.PreventsTileRemovalIfOnTopOfIt[target.type])
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		if (IsLockedDoor(target))
		{
			return true;
		}
		if (scanForContainer && IsAContainer(target))
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool DefaultTreeWallTest(int wallType)
	{
		if (wallType >= 0 && wallType < WallID.Count && WallID.Sets.AllowsPlantsToGrow[wallType])
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool GemTreeWallTest(int wallType)
	{
		if (DefaultTreeWallTest(wallType))
		{
			return true;
		}
		switch (wallType)
		{
		case 2:
		case 54:
		case 55:
		case 56:
		case 57:
		case 58:
		case 59:
		case 61:
		case 185:
		case 196:
		case 197:
		case 198:
		case 199:
		case 208:
		case 209:
		case 210:
		case 211:
		case 212:
		case 213:
		case 214:
		case 215:
			return true;
		default:
			return false;
		}
	}

	public static bool GemTreeGroundTest(int tileType)
	{
		if (tileType < 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (TileID.Sets.Conversion.Stone[tileType] || TileID.Sets.Conversion.Moss[tileType])
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool VanityTreeGroundTest(int tileType)
	{
		if (tileType < 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (TileID.Sets.Conversion.Grass[tileType] && tileType != 23 && tileType != 199)
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool AshTreeGroundTest(int tileType)
	{
		if (tileType < 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (tileType == 633)
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool TryGrowingTreeByType(int treeTileType, int checkedX, int checkedY, int treeHeightAddon = 0, bool ignoreWalls = false)
	{
		bool result = false;
		switch (treeTileType)
		{
		case 5:
			result = GrowTree(checkedX, checkedY, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 323:
			result = GrowPalmTree(checkedX, checkedY, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 587:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Ruby, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 588:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Diamond, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 583:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Topaz, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 584:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Amethyst, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 589:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Amber, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 586:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Emerald, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 585:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Sapphire, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 596:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.VanityTree_Sakura, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 616:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.VanityTree_Willow, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 634:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.Tree_Ash, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public static void TileFrame(int i, int j, bool resetFrame = false, bool noBreak = false)
	{
		if (i <= 5 || j <= 5 || i >= Main.maxTilesX - 5 || j >= Main.maxTilesY - 5 || Main.tile[i, j] == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		MapUpdateQueue.Add(i, j);
		try
		{
			Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j];
			if (!tile.active())
			{
				tile.halfBrick(halfBrick: false);
				tile.ClearBlockPaintAndCoating();
				tile.slope(0);
			}
			if (tile.liquid > 0 && Main.netMode != 1 && !generatingWorld)
			{
				Liquid.AddWater(i, j);
			}
			if (!tile.active())
			{
				return;
			}
			int type = tile.type;
			if (Main.tileFrameImportant[type])

				if (!noBreak || TileID.Sets.Torches[type] || TileID.Sets.FrameImportantEchoCulling[type])
				{
					TileFrameImportant(i, j, type, tile, resetFrame);

					}
					break;

				if (TileID.Sets.IsVine[type] && !CheckVines(i, j, type, tile))

	private static bool SpawnFallingBlockProjectile(int i, int j, int type, Tile tileCache)
	{
		if (Main.netMode == 1)
		{
			return false;
		}
		Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j - 1];
		Tile tile2 = Main.tile[i, j + 1];
		if (tileCache == null || tile == null || tile2 == null)

		}

	}

	public static void SquareTileFrame(int i, int j, bool resetFrame = true)
	{
		TileFrame(i - 1, j - 1);
		TileFrame(i - 1, j);
		TileFrame(i - 1, j + 1);
		TileFrame(i, j - 1);
		TileFrame(i, j, resetFrame);
		TileFrame(i, j + 1);
		TileFrame(i + 1, j - 1);
		TileFrame(i + 1, j);
		TileFrame(i + 1, j + 1);
	}

	public static void SquareWallFrame(int i, int j, bool resetFrame = true)
	{
		Framing.WallFrame(i - 1, j - 1);
		Framing.WallFrame(i - 1, j);
		Framing.WallFrame(i - 1, j + 1);
		Framing.WallFrame(i, j - 1);
		Framing.WallFrame(i, j, resetFrame);
		Framing.WallFrame(i, j + 1);
		Framing.WallFrame(i + 1, j - 1);
		Framing.WallFrame(i + 1, j);
		Framing.WallFrame(i + 1, j + 1);
	}

			for (int j = sectionY; j <= sectionY2; j++)
			{
				if (Main.sectionManager.SectionLoaded(i, j) && !Main.sectionManager.SectionFramed(i, j))
				{
					SectionTileFrame(i, j, i, j);
					if (!Main.sectionManager.AnyUnfinishedSections)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

This is the full CheckTree cascade. The key part for our case:

```csharp
if (num4 != 2 && num4 != type && ((frameX == 0 && frameY <= 130) || (frameX == 22 && frameY <= 130) || (frameX == 44 && frameY <= 130)))
{
    KillTile(i, j);
}
```

num4 = type of tile BELOW. When the trunk base is killed by Flowers, the trunk tile above it: below is now inactive (num4 = -1 ≠ 2, ≠ 5) and its frame (trunk middle tiles have frameX ∈ {0,22,44}, frameY ≤ 130 mostly) → KillTile(i, j) → kills that trunk tile → and each KillTile ends with SquareTileFrame → recursion up the whole tree. THE WHOLE TREE COLLAPSES. Then PlaceTile(m, n, 3) places a plant at the base position (which is now empty air).

Wait — actually careful: the Flowers body order: KillTile(m, n) [kills trunk base → whole tree cascade-collapse happens INSIDE this call, via SquareTileFrame at the end of each KillTile], then slope reset on tile5, then PlaceTile(m, n, 3) → plant at the old base spot. Then the "if (tile.active && type == 3) → maybe type 73".

Hmm wait — but there's a subtlety: does the cascade actually happen? KillTile(trunk base at (m,n)) → at the end: `SquareTileFrame(i, j)` → TileFrame(m, n-1) [trunk above] → TileFrameImportant → CheckTree(m, n-1) → below (m, n) inactive → num4=-1 → for a middle trunk tile with frameX==0/22/44 && frameY<=130 → KillTile(m, n-1) → ... → SquareTileFrame(m, n-1) → TileFrame(m, n-2)... recursive cascade → whole tree dies. YES. And the tree "root side tiles" (frameX 66/88 branches) also die via their checks.

So in vanilla, the sequence for a Flowers-overlapped tree:
1. KillTile(base) → base tile gone → cascade kills ALL tree tiles.
2. PlaceTile(3) at base → plant there (2/3 chance upgraded to type 73 via the frameX reassign + type=73).

160	/** OreHelper（cs:10192）：矿块 ±1 邻域的石头(1)/黏土(40) 改回泥土(0) */
161	function oreHelper(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {
162	  for (let i = x - 1; i <= x + 1; i++) {
163	    for (let j = y - 1; j <= y + 1; j++) {
164	      if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;
165	      const s = SHEET[st.type[st.idx(i, j)]];
166	      if (s === 1 || s === 40) st.type[st.idx(i, j)] = TILE_BY_KEY['dirt']!;
167	    }
168	  }
169	}
170	
171	/** OrePatch（cs:10577）1:1：草面露头矿脉（先竖走丝带后随机游走团） */
172	function orePatch(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, X: number, Y: number): boolean {
173	  let type = gs.oreTiers.copper;
174	  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) type = gs.oreTiers.iron;                    // Next(3)==0 → 铁
175	  let num = X, num2 = Y;
176	  while (!solidTile(st, num, num2)) {
177	    num2++;
178	    if (num2 > gs.worldSurface) return false;
179	  }
180	  if (!CONV_GRASS.has(sheetAt(st, num, num2)) || !CONV_GRASS.has(sheetAt(st, num - 1, num2))
181	    || !CONV_GRASS.has(sheetAt(st, num + 1, num2))) return false;
182	  if (!st.inBounds(num, num2) || st.wall[st.idx(num, num2)] > 0) return false;
183	  for (let i = num - 10; i <= num + 10; i++) {
184	    for (let j = num2 + 7; j <= num2 + 30; j++) {
185	      if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return false;                            // Main.tile 越界 = null
186	      const k = st.idx(i, j);
187	      const s = SHEET[st.type[k]];
188	      if (!st.flags[k] || TILE_DUNGEON.has(s) || CLOUDS.has(s) || CONV_SAND.has(s) || st.wall[k] === 0) {
189	        return false;
190	      }
191	    }
192	  }
193	  const setOre = (x: number, y: number): void => {
194	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
195	    const k = st.idx(x, y);
196	    st.type[k] = type;
197	    st.flags[k] = 1;                                                   // 仅 type+active（原版不清半砖/坡）
198	  };
199	  num2 += rng.int(0, 1);                                               // Next(2)
200	  setOre(num, num2);
201	  oreHelper(st, num, num2);
202	  const num3 = num2;
203	  // 注意：C# while 条件里的 genRand.Next(8,13) 每圈重掷——1:1 保留
204	  while (num2 < num3 + rng.int(8, 12)) {
205	    num += rng.int(-1, 1);                                             // Next(-1,2)
206	    num2 += rng.int(1, 2);                                             // Next(1,3)
207	    if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) num2++;
208	    setOre(num, num2);
209	    oreHelper(st, num, num2);
210	    if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) {                                         // Next(4)
211	      const n4 = num + rng.int(-2, 2);                                 // Next(-2,3)
212	      const n5 = num2 + rng.int(1, 1);                                 // Next(2)
213	      setOre(n4, n5);
214	      oreHelper(st, n4, n5);
215	    }
216	  }
217	  // 随机游走团（cs:10659-10712）
218	  let vx = num, vy = num2;
219	  let v2x = rng.next() * 0.6 - 0.3;
220	  let v2y = rng.next() * 0.5 + 0.5;
221	  let n6 = rng.int(5, 8);                                              // Next(5,9)
222	  let n7 = rng.int(9, 13);                                             // Next(9,14)
223	  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) n6 += rng.int(1, 1);                        // Next(2)
224	  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) n7 += rng.int(1, 1);
225	  while (n7 > 0) {
226	    n7--;
227	    const lo = (n6 * 4) | 0;
228	    for (let k = num - lo; k <= num + n6 * 4; k++) {
229	      for (let l = num2 - lo; l <= num2 + n6 * 4; l++) {
230	        const n8 = n6 * (0.5 + rng.next() * 0.5) * 0.1;
231	        let n9 = n6 * (0.7 + rng.next() * 0.6) * 0.3;
232	        if (rng.int(0, 7) === 0) n9 *= 2;                              // Next(8)
233	        if (!st.inBounds(k, l)) continue;
234	        const dx = vx - k, dy = vy - l;
235	        const d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
236	        const kk = st.idx(k, l);
237	        if (d < n8) {
238	          st.flags[kk] = 0;                                            // 挖空（留幽灵 type）
239	        } else if (d < n9) {
240	          st.type[kk] = type;
241	          if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) st.flags[kk] = 1;                   // Next(4)
242	          oreHelper(st, k, l);
243	        }
244	      }
245	    }
246	    vx += v2x; vy += v2y;
247	    v2x += rng.next() * 0.2 - 0.1;
248	    v2y += rng.next() * 0.2 - 0.1;
249	    // 原版此处的 Utils.Clamp(...) 是无赋值语句（返回值被丢弃）——不夹紧，1:1 保留
250	  }
251	  return true;
252	}
